### 5.4 Step 4: Check your visa is real

1. <a id="step-4-check-your-visa-is-real-item-1"></a>**Track it** on [visa.educationmalaysia.gov.my](https://visa.educationmalaysia.gov.my/): Student Visa, Track My Application, enter passport number and nationality, tick the terms box, press Track My Application <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>. IMI's own status page sends Student Pass checks to EMGS <sup>[188](#o188)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-4-check-your-visa-is-real-item-2"></a>**At 70%** Immigration has approved your eVAL; download it as a PDF from your EMGS account <sup>[N166](#n166)</sup>. If progress stops, ask the institution's international office, which must confirm your application, or EMGS via [educationmalaysia.gov.my/get-in-touch/contact-us](https://educationmalaysia.gov.my/get-in-touch/contact-us) <sup>[184 (2019)](#o184), [189](#o189)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How can you check that an eVAL document itself is genuine?

<a id="after-you-arrive-1"></a>

### 5.5 After you arrive

1. <a id="after-you-arrive-1-item-1"></a>**Register the MDAC** (Malaysia Digital Arrival Card) within 3 days before arriving (see [Tourist visa, Step 4: Fill in the Malaysia Digital Arrival Card (MDAC)](#step-4-fill-in-the-malaysia-digital-arrival-card-mdac)) <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>; long-term pass holders are exempt <sup>[126](#o126)</sup>.
2. <a id="after-you-arrive-1-item-2"></a>**At Kuala Lumpur International Airport (KLIA),** go to EMGS's 24-hour International Student Arrival Centre (ISAC) before immigration (Terminal 1: Level 3 arrival hall; Terminal 2: Level 3A after the Skybridge); its staff help you through immigration, with priority lanes for students <sup>[190](#o190)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Which documents does ISAC ask to see?
   > - Does ISAC charge anything?

3. <a id="after-you-arrive-1-item-3"></a>**Within 7 working days do the medical** at an EMGS panel clinic, listed on EMGS's site and app; without it EMGS can't start your pass <sup>[191](#o191), [192](#o192)</sup>. If you fail, appeal at the same clinic within 3 days of being told <sup>[53](#o53)</sup>; if that fails, ask for any refund within 90 days of EMGS's notice <sup>[182](#o182)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How do you book the medical?
   > - What does the medical appeal cost?
   > - Which documents does the medical appeal need?
   > - What are the medical appeal's possible outcomes?

4. <a id="after-you-arrive-1-item-4"></a>**The institution gets your pass sticker** with your original passport, its letter, EMGS's support letter, your stamped personal bond and at least 12 months' Malaysian health insurance <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>; IMI's client charter gives 5 working days <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.

<a id="how-long-you-can-stay"></a>

### 5.6 How long you can stay

The pass usually lasts up to a year; renew as early as 3 months before expiry <sup>[53](#o53)</sup>, yourself or through the institution, on STARS (EMGS's Student Application Registration System) or the EMGS website <sup>[52](#o52), [53](#o53)</sup>. You'll need:

- a 35 x 45 mm photo on white <sup>[181](#o181)</sup>;
- copies of your passport's data, visa and observation pages <sup>[181](#o181)</sup>;
- your certificates and transcripts <sup>[181](#o181)</sup>.

You need a CGPA (grade point average) of at least 2.0 and 80% attendance, or an explanation letter from the institution <sup>[181](#o181)</sup>. Renewal costs RM270 to EMGS (private institution) and RM60 to IMI <sup>[172](#o172), [52](#o52)</sup>. Breaking any condition can get the pass cancelled <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long does a renewal take?

**Changing course:** the institution may file a Variation application once, within 18 months after your first semester, if you finished at least one semester at bachelor's level or above; otherwise it ends your pass with a Check-Out Memo and you leave Malaysia before applying again <sup>[53](#o53)</sup>. With an eVAL but no pass yet, you must leave to change course <sup>[53](#o53)</sup>.

<a id="can-you-work"></a>

### 5.7 Can you work?

The rules:

- **Who:** only university and private higher education students, not language or training centre students <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.
- **When:** in semester breaks or holidays over 7 days <sup>[52](#o52), [53](#o53)</sup>.
- **How much:** at most 20 hours a week <sup>[52](#o52), [53](#o53)</sup>.
- **Where:** restaurants, petrol stations, mini markets, hotels or on campus <sup>[52](#o52), [53](#o53)</sup>.
- **Never:** as a cashier, front desk worker, hotel singer, masseur, musician or guest relations officer, or student recruiter <sup>[52](#o52), [53](#o53)</sup>.

Students working outside these rules are deported, sometimes fined or jailed <sup>[N101](#n101)</sup>. Claims of an agreement giving Bangladeshi students graduate passes to work are false <sup>[N167](#n167)</sup>.

To get permission first, you'll need:

- the job offer letter <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- the institution's supporting letter with the break dates <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- your results and attendance report <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- copies of your data page and Student Pass <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- the employer's Labour Department (JTK) approval to employ a foreigner under section 60K of the Employment Act 1955 <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

Go in person with an institution representative to IMI's Student Pass Unit at headquarters or the state Immigration office, which decides after checking the documents <sup>[53](#o53), [52](#o52)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the fee for work permission?
> - How long does a decision take?

<a id="bringing-your-family-1"></a>

### 5.8 Bringing your family

Only master's and PhD students may bring dependants (spouse, children under 18, disabled children of any age, parents and parents-in-law); a Bangladeshi bachelor's student can't, and dependants may not work <sup>[52](#o52), [53](#o53)</sup>. The institution applies on STARS <sup>[52](#o52), [53](#o53)</sup>. You'll need:

- Forms IMM.12 and IMM.38 <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- the institution's letters requesting the pass and confirming your Student Pass <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- a copy of every page of the dependant's passport, cover included (valid 18 months or more), and of your data page and pass <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- a 3.5 x 5.0 cm photo on white <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- 12 months' health insurance <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- a stamped personal bond (new applications) <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- proof of support, such as 3 months' bank statements or a scholarship letter <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- the marriage or birth certificate <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

Bring the originals; incomplete applications aren't processed <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. Bangla documents need a certified translation; a dependant already in Malaysia needs relationship documents certified by the Bangladesh High Commission <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. The costs:

- EMGS: RM250 per dependant (RM140 to renew) <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>;
- insurance, bought by each dependant <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>;
- IMI: RM90 for the Long-Term Social Visit Pass <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

That pass has a category for close family of a foreign student <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>. For a child born in Malaysia <sup>[156](#o156)</sup>, see [Having a child in Malaysia](#having-a-child-in-malaysia).

> **Need to know**
> - How long does a dependant's application take?
> - How is the result shown?
> - Does the close-family category cover a bachelor's student's family?

<a id="if-the-visa-is-refused"></a>

### 5.9 If the visa is refused

<a id="scams-1"></a>

### 5.10 Scams

<a id="done-to-students"></a>

#### 5.10.1 Done to students

Besides the traps in [Step 1: Check the institution and the course](#step-1-check-the-institution-and-the-course) and [Step 2: Apply from Bangladesh](#step-2-apply-from-bangladesh), agents claim a student visa lets you work freely; it doesn't <sup>[N101](#n101), [52](#o52)</sup>. Agents have charged families Tk 6 to 8 lakh or more for admission and a visa, steering students to barely functioning colleges <sup>[N101](#n101)</sup>; agencies have left EMGS applications stuck for months and, after arrival, moved students from the promised university into a Bangladeshi-owned English language centre, whose students can't work <sup>[N165](#n165)</sup>.

<a id="tried-by-applicants"></a>

#### 5.10.2 Tried by applicants

Getting a pass by a false statement, or using someone else's, carries a fine up to RM10,000 or up to 5 years' jail <sup>[39 (2006)](#o39)</sup>.

**If an agent cheated you or holds your passport,** in this order:

1. <a id="tried-by-applicants-item-1"></a>**Bangladesh police.** Register on [gd.police.gov.bd](https://gd.police.gov.bd/) or the Online GD app with your NID, a mobile number (for a one-time code) and a live photo; pick the district and station, describe what happened, when and where, and attach receipts and messages <sup>[104](#o104)</sup>. A GD (General Diary) comes back as a digital copy with its number and the investigating officer's details; for a case, take the printout or complaint code to that station <sup>[104](#o104)</sup>. Or call the CID (Criminal Investigation Department) hotline, 01320-019999, or control room, 01320-019998, which wants to hear of anyone holding passports or demanding unusual visa sums <sup>[195](#o195), [N97](#n97)</sup>.
2. <a id="tried-by-applicants-item-2"></a>**The Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare's Complaint Resolution Cell,** minister.expat@gmail.com <sup>[196](#o196)</sup>. Sample: "My son [name, passport no.] paid [agency, address] Tk [amount] on [dates] for admission to [institution] in Malaysia. The fees were not paid and the agency holds his passport. Receipts attached. Please help recover the passport and money."

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does the Ministry act on complaints about education agents, which hold no recruiting licence?

3. <a id="tried-by-applicants-item-3"></a>**In Malaysia,** the Bangladesh High Commission's consular wing: consular.kualalumpur@gmail.com, +603 2604 0946, mission.kualalumpur@mofa.gov.bd, No 8, Lorong Yap Kwan Seng, 50450 Kuala Lumpur <sup>[107](#o107), [125](#o125)</sup>. In an emergency call 999 (free) for the Royal Malaysia Police <sup>[197](#o197)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does the High Commission help students recover passports held by agents?

4. <a id="tried-by-applicants-item-4"></a>**Against the institution,** KPT's SiSPAA complaints system, [mohe.spab.gov.my](https://mohe.spab.gov.my/eApps/system/index.do) <sup>[198](#o198), [199](#o199)</sup>: register ("Daftar Baharu"), log in, choose "Aduan/Maklum Balas Baharu"; "Semak Aduan/Maklum Balas" shows progress <sup>[199](#o199), [200](#o200)</sup>. Give full name, passport number, phone and email, and attach the offer letter and receipts, or KPT may reject it; it doesn't take private money disputes with agents <sup>[199](#o199)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How long does KPT take to reply?

5. <a id="tried-by-applicants-item-5"></a>**Free legal help:** OKUP (Ovibashi Karmi Unnayan Program, a Bangladeshi migrants' organisation) advises and files cases for migrant workers, families and trafficking survivors: helpline 01842773300, 4th Floor, 466 Sawdagar Garden, Dania Post Office Road, Dhaka 1236 <sup>[N168](#n168)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does OKUP take the cases of cheated students?

<a id="school-pupils"></a>

### 5.11 School pupils

IMI gives Student Passes to foreign pre-school and school children <sup>[52](#o52)</sup> with a place at:

- a government or government-aided school under KPM <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- an international, expatriate, private or religious school or Chinese independent secondary school registered with KPM's Private Education Division; an international school also needs KDN's permission <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

The school's representative, a parent or a legal guardian applies, new or renewal, at IMI's Student Pass Unit at headquarters, the state Visa, Pass and Permit Division, or a branch offering Student Passes, by the school's address <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. You'll need:

- Forms IMM.14 and IMM.38 <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- a copy of every page of the child's passport, cover included (renewal: data page and current pass), valid 18 months or more <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- a 3.5 x 5.0 cm photo on white <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- 12 months' health insurance <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- a stamped personal bond (new) <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- for a government or aided school, the state or district education office's approval letter <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>;
- the originals <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

It costs RM60 plus the visa fee <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>; IMI's client charter gives 5 working days <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>. The child's parents, legal guardian or adoptive parents, and siblings under 7 may get a Long-Term Social Visit Pass as companions, at RM90, with no work or business <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which extra documents do international and private schools need?
> - Can a refusal be appealed?

<a id="tourist-visa-social-visit"></a>

## 6 Tourist visa (social visit)

Bangladeshis need an eVISA to visit for tourism, family or short business, and may stay up to 30 days <sup>[3](#o3), [61](#o61)</sup>. Ordinary visits get a single-entry visa; multiple-entry is only for business, medical treatment, Fly & Cruise and wedding tourism <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>. It never allows work or study (see [Can you work?](#can-you-work-1)) <sup>[201](#o201)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-prepare"></a>

### 6.1 Step 1: Prepare

You'll need:

- a passport valid over 6 months from travel, with at least 3 blank pages <sup>[4](#o4), [3](#o3)</sup>;
- a studio photo, 35 x 50 mm, white background, full face, under 6 months old, no selfies or scans <sup>[3](#o3), [4](#o4)</sup>;
- scans of the data page and the page with your previous passport number <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>;
- a confirmed return ticket <sup>[4](#o4), [3](#o3)</sup>;
- proof of paid accommodation, or, staying with friends or relatives, a cover letter to the immigration officer with your details, the address, and the host's name, Malaysian identity card number and phone <sup>[4](#o4), [3](#o3)</sup>;
- proof of money (cash, card or e-money backed by 3 months' bank statements) <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>;
- for a child, the birth certificate <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>;
- a credit card, or Alipay or WeChat Pay, to pay <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>.

Merge several files for one upload field into one <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>.

<a id="step-2-apply-on-myvisa"></a>

### 6.2 Step 2: Apply on MyVISA

1. <a id="step-2-apply-on-myvisa-item-1"></a>**Use only [malaysiavisa.imi.gov.my](https://malaysiavisa.imi.gov.my/),** IMI's only official eVISA site; you can't apply from inside Malaysia, and third-party applications can't be verified <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>. If an agent applies, only the agent sees the status, and IMI takes no responsibility <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>; agents in Bangladesh charge Tk 5,000 to 8,000 for an eVISA you can apply for yourself <sup>[N169](#n169)</sup>. MyVISA has shut for weeks at a time (from 10 February 2026 and again in July 2026), suspending all applications and approvals until a notice says it is back <sup>[N170](#n170), [202](#o202), [203](#o203)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-apply-on-myvisa-item-2"></a>**Open an account:** under Individual, Sign Up, fill in the form and CAPTCHA (picture code); change the emailed temporary password at first login <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>. Log in with email, the emailed 6-digit code and password <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>. One account holds 5 applications, for up to 5 people <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-2-apply-on-myvisa-item-3"></a>**Apply on a weekday, 9 am to 5 pm Bangladesh time** (the only hours open to Bangladesh) <sup>[204](#o204)</sup>, at least 14 days before travel <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>. Write your name exactly as in your passport; with no surname, leave Surname blank or put the full name in Given Name <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>. Unpaid drafts expire after 7 working days <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-2-apply-on-myvisa-item-4"></a>**Pay** RM20 visa fee, RM105 processing and 0.8% (card) or 1.7% (e-wallet) convenience charge; nothing is refunded, even on refusal <sup>[3](#o3), [185](#o185)</sup>. Visa agencies add their own fee <sup>[61](#o61)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-2-apply-on-myvisa-item-5"></a>**Watch the status** <sup>[201](#o201)</sup>:
   - "Document Submission or Information Update Required": upload what is asked <sup>[201](#o201)</sup>.
   - "Interview Requested": confirm and attend, online or in person as the High Commission sets <sup>[201](#o201)</sup>.
   - No response in 5 days means automatic rejection <sup>[201](#o201)</sup>.

   A decision takes 2 to 7 working days, restarting after any re-upload <sup>[3](#o3), [201](#o201)</sup>.
6. <a id="step-2-apply-on-myvisa-item-6"></a>**Download and print the eVISA** (Actions, Download eVISA) <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>. It allows one journey within 6 months of issue, whatever the travel date in that window <sup>[201](#o201), [3](#o3)</sup>; it cancels any earlier visa still active <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>. A wrong detail from your own mistake means applying and paying again <sup>[201](#o201)</sup>.
7. <a id="step-2-apply-on-myvisa-item-7"></a>**If refused,** there is no appeal, and no reason need be given; you can reapply and pay again <sup>[3](#o3), [51](#o51)</sup>. Questions and complaints: High Commission of Malaysia, Dhaka, dhaka@imi.gov.my (visa section), +88 02 4108 1918 (visa), House 19, Road 6, Baridhara Diplomatic Enclave, Sunday to Thursday 8.30 am to 4.30 pm <sup>[51](#o51), [108](#o108)</sup>.

<a id="step-3-check-your-visa-is-real"></a>

### 6.3 Step 3: Check your visa is real

At [malaysiavisa.imi.gov.my/evisa/check-evisa](https://malaysiavisa.imi.gov.my/evisa/check-evisa) enter your passport number, the visa sticker number and the CAPTCHA <sup>[205](#o205)</sup>; only official-site applications can be checked <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What does a genuine eVISA look like?
> - Where is its sticker number printed?

<a id="step-4-fill-in-the-malaysia-digital-arrival-card-mdac"></a>

### 6.4 Step 4: Fill in the Malaysia Digital Arrival Card (MDAC)

1. <a id="step-4-fill-in-the-malaysia-digital-arrival-card-mdac-item-1"></a>**Submit it within 3 days before arrival,** the submission day included <sup>[4](#o4), [129](#o129)</sup>, only at [imigresen-online.imi.gov.my/mdac/main](https://imigresen-online.imi.gov.my/mdac/main), which lists no fee <sup>[128](#o128)</sup>. Fake sites charge, such as malaysiacard.com (USD 29.99), and appear as sponsored search results <sup>[127 (2024)](#o127), [N141](#n141)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-4-fill-in-the-malaysia-digital-arrival-card-mdac-item-2"></a>**Fill in** your name, passport number, date and place of birth, nationality, sex, passport expiry, email, mobile, arrival and departure dates, flight number, mode of travel and last airport of departure <sup>[129](#o129)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-4-fill-in-the-malaysia-digital-arrival-card-mdac-item-3"></a>**Check it** under "Check Registration" with passport number, nationality and PIN <sup>[206](#o206)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-4-fill-in-the-malaysia-digital-arrival-card-mdac-item-4"></a>**Show it** with your passport at immigration <sup>[128](#o128)</sup>.

<a id="step-5-at-the-border"></a>

### 6.5 Step 5: At the border

**At Dhaka airport,** immigration police stop travellers (Malaysia included) whose visa, BMET clearance (a worker's emigration clearance), papers or answers don't match <sup>[N171](#n171)</sup>. Carry what Malaysia checks: eVISA, return ticket, paid accommodation and proof of money <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What should you do if Bangladeshi immigration stops you?
> - Can you complain about being stopped?
> - Can you travel later after being stopped?

**At the Malaysian airport,** the officer checks passport, eVISA, MDAC, return ticket and paid accommodation <sup>[3](#o3), [128](#o128)</sup>. A visa doesn't guarantee entry: you can be refused if you can't explain your plans, or can't prove you aren't a "prohibited immigrant", which includes lacking means of support <sup>[3](#o3), [51](#o51), [207](#o207), [39 (2006)](#o39)</sup>. Autogates aren't open to Bangladeshis <sup>[59](#o59)</sup>. Bangladeshis holding valid tourist visas are regularly turned back at KLIA for fake hotel bookings, no return ticket, too little money or no satisfactory reason <sup>[N172](#n172), [N173](#n173), [N98](#n98)</sup>; travellers are told to carry RM500 to RM1,000, and officers may check phone photos <sup>[N174](#n174)</sup>. Those refused go on a "Not to Land" list and the airline returns them within about 24 hours <sup>[N105](#n105), [N175 (2023)](#n175)</sup>.

**If refused entry:** the Immigration Act's appeal to the Minister covers only Permit holders, not visitors <sup>[39 (2006)](#o39)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a refused visitor appeal?
> - Is there an official minimum sum of money?
> - What does "Not to Land" mean for future visits?

<a id="can-you-work-1"></a>

### 6.6 Can you work?

A visit pass allows no work, work training or study <sup>[201](#o201), [51](#o51)</sup> and lapses once its conditions are broken <sup>[39 (2006)](#o39)</sup>. Overstaying brings fines and can bar re-entry for a set period <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>. A visit pass is extended only for illness, an accident or war at home, with evidence and a return ticket, by applying in person on Form IMM.55 <sup>[208](#o208)</sup>. A directive of 8 June 2026 ordered a nationwide multi-agency crackdown on people working on tourist, student and other short passes <sup>[N176](#n176)</sup>.

<a id="if-the-visa-is-refused-1"></a>

### 6.7 If the visa is refused

<a id="scams-2"></a>

### 6.8 Scams

<a id="done-to-travellers"></a>

#### 6.8.1 Done to travellers

Fraudsters bring Bangladeshi women on tourist visas promising jobs, though no agreement exists to recruit Bangladeshi women for Malaysia <sup>[N21](#n21)</sup>. Rings in Kuala Lumpur sell Bangladeshis forged European visas for up to RM20,000 <sup>[N177](#n177)</sup>; a man promised Italy was sent with fake Malaysian and French visas and turned back at the Malaysian airport <sup>[N178](#n178)</sup>. Travel agencies have promised work permits, sent people on visit visas, held them and demanded ransom from families <sup>[N97](#n97)</sup>.

<a id="tried-by-travellers"></a>

#### 6.8.2 Tried by travellers

Getting a pass by a false statement carries a fine up to RM10,000 or up to 5 years' jail <sup>[39 (2006)](#o39)</sup>.

**If someone sold you a "job on a tourist visa", holds your passport, or holds you in Malaysia,** in this order:

1. <a id="tried-by-travellers-item-1"></a>**Held or in danger in Malaysia:** call 999 for the Royal Malaysia Police (PDRM) <sup>[197](#o197)</sup>, then the Bangladesh High Commission's consular wing, +603 2604 0946, consular.kualalumpur@gmail.com <sup>[107](#o107)</sup>.
2. <a id="tried-by-travellers-item-2"></a>**Family calls the Probashi call centre** of WEWB (the Wage Earners' Welfare Board, the government body for migrant workers' welfare), for problems of migrants and families: 16135 toll free in Bangladesh, +880 9610102030 from abroad, 24 hours <sup>[76](#o76)</sup>.
3. <a id="tried-by-travellers-item-3"></a>**Bangladesh police:** online at [gd.police.gov.bd](https://gd.police.gov.bd/) or at the station (see Student visa (Student Pass), [Tried by applicants, item 1: Bangladesh police](#tried-by-applicants-item-1)), or the CID hotline, 01320-019999, for anyone holding passports or demanding unusual visa sums <sup>[104](#o104), [195](#o195), [N97](#n97)</sup>.
4. <a id="tried-by-travellers-item-4"></a>**Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare and Overseas Employment** (Probashi Kallyan Bhaban, 71-72 Old Elephant Road, Dhaka-1000) <sup>[101](#o101)</sup>:

   - **Against a recruiting agency:** email the enforcement branch, sasenforcement@probashi.gov.bd (02-41030255), with your proof, full address and the agency's RL (licence) number and address; free, action within 3 to 5 working days, settled within 30 <sup>[100](#o100)</sup>.
   - **Against a licensed agency that sent you abroad without permission:** apply to the Secretary with your BMET smart card, ticket, contract and passport copies; free, settled within 60 working days of BMET's recommendation <sup>[100](#o100)</sup>.
   - **Anything else:** the Complaint Resolution Cell, minister.expat@gmail.com <sup>[196](#o196)</sup>.

   Sample: "I, [name, passport no.], paid [agent, address, phone] Tk [amount] on [date] for a job in Malaysia. I was sent on a tourist visa on [date] and [what happened]. Receipts and messages attached."
5. <a id="tried-by-travellers-item-5"></a>**Free legal help:** OKUP (see Student visa (Student Pass), [Tried by applicants, item 5: Free legal help](#tried-by-applicants-item-5)) helps trafficking survivors and migrants' families, helpline 01842773300 <sup>[N168](#n168)</sup>.

<a id="family-visit-sponsored-by-a-malaysian-citizen-vdr"></a>

## 7 Family visit sponsored by a Malaysian citizen (VDR)

A Malaysian citizen (the sponsor) can invite a Bangladeshi relative through a VDR (Visa Dengan Rujukan, visa with reference): the sponsor applies in Malaysia, Immigration headquarters approves, and a Malaysian mission issues the visa <sup>[209](#o209), [210 (2023)](#o210), [6](#o6)</sup>. Proof of relationship:

- **Parents or parents-in-law:** the sponsor's spouse's marriage certificate, birth certificate and passport, plus a notarial certificate endorsed by the Malaysian mission and Wisma Putra (Malaysia's foreign ministry) <sup>[209](#o209)</sup>.
- **A child under 7:** the birth certificate and the sponsor's marriage certificate <sup>[209](#o209)</sup>.
- **A spouse:** the marriage certificate <sup>[209](#o209)</sup>.
- **Other relatives:** documentary proof <sup>[209](#o209)</sup>.

No work, work training or study <sup>[51](#o51)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-send-your-papers-to-the-sponsor"></a>

### 7.1 Step 1: Send your papers to the sponsor

You'll need:

- a passport valid more than 6 months from travel, every printed page scanned <sup>[210 (2023)](#o210), [3](#o3)</sup>;
- 2 photos, 3.5 x 5.0 cm <sup>[211](#o211), [210 (2023)](#o210)</sup>;
- the relationship documents above <sup>[209](#o209)</sup>;
- for the sponsor's form, your marital history, job in Bangladesh with proof, earlier visits to and work in Malaysia, spouse and children, address and planned length of stay <sup>[210 (2023)](#o210)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-1-send-your-papers-to-the-sponsor-item-1"></a>**Parents or parents-in-law: get the notarial certificate endorsed** <sup>[209](#o209)</sup> at the High Commission of Malaysia in Dhaka (contacts in [Tourist visa, Step 2, item 7](#step-2-apply-on-myvisa-item-7)) and at Wisma Putra. Wisma Putra's Consular Division takes documents walk-in from the person or a representative with ID, usually ready the same or next working day: Wisma Putra No 1, Jalan Wisma Putra, Precinct 2, 62602 Putrajaya; 03-8000 8000; sah_admin@kln.gov.my; Monday to Thursday 8.30 am to 4 pm, Friday 8.30 to 12 and 2.45 to 4 pm <sup>[212 (2023)](#o212)</sup>. Non-English documents need an English translation <sup>[212 (2023)](#o212)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What must the certificate state?
   > - Which notary may make it?
   > - What are the endorsement fees in Dhaka and Putrajaya?
   > - How long does endorsement take in Dhaka and Putrajaya?
   > - Must the Bangladesh High Commission in Kuala Lumpur authenticate it first (Wisma Putra's general rule for documents from abroad) <sup>[212 (2023)](#o212)</sup>?
   > - Can the sponsor take it to Wisma Putra?

2. <a id="step-1-send-your-papers-to-the-sponsor-item-2"></a>**Send the sponsor copies of everything,** with your name in capitals, sex, place and country of birth, date of birth, nationality and passport details exactly as in your passport, for Form Imm.12 <sup>[211](#o211)</sup>. Ask the sponsor to name the High Commission in Dhaka as the issuing mission <sup>[210 (2023)](#o210), [108](#o108)</sup>.

<a id="step-2-the-sponsor-applies-in-malaysia"></a>

### 7.2 Step 2: The sponsor applies in Malaysia

You'll need (the sponsor):

- MyKad (Malaysian identity card) <sup>[210 (2023)](#o210)</sup>;
- the 3 latest payslips showing RM2,000 a month or more, or, if self-employed, the SSM (Companies Commission of Malaysia) business registration and 3 months' bank statements <sup>[210 (2023)](#o210), [209](#o209)</sup>;
- an employer's confirmation letter <sup>[210 (2023)](#o210), [209](#o209)</sup>;
- the papers from [Step 1: Send your papers to the sponsor](#step-1-send-your-papers-to-the-sponsor) <sup>[210 (2023)](#o210)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-2-the-sponsor-applies-in-malaysia-item-1"></a>**Download the forms** from [IMI's VDR checklist](https://www.imi.gov.my/wp-content/uploads/2023/08/SENARAI-SEMAK-Borang-VDR.pdf): Form Imm.12, the sponsor's declaration (Surat Akuan Penaja), the bond and the sponsor information form (Borang Maklumat Tambahan Penaja) <sup>[210 (2023)](#o210)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-the-sponsor-applies-in-malaysia-item-2"></a>**Type Form Imm.12** in 2 copies, with the relative's photo on each <sup>[211](#o211), [209](#o209)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-2-the-sponsor-applies-in-malaysia-item-3"></a>**Swear the declaration** (job, employer, monthly income and full responsibility for the relative) before a Commissioner for Oaths at a government court, a Sessions Court judge, magistrate or notary public <sup>[210 (2023)](#o210)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-2-the-sponsor-applies-in-malaysia-item-4"></a>**Sign a personal bond, stamped RM10 at LHDN** (the Inland Revenue Board); the sponsor forfeits its sum if the relative breaks the visit's conditions <sup>[209](#o209), [213](#o213)</sup>. A cash security bond applies only to citizens of China and Nigeria <sup>[209](#o209)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-2-the-sponsor-applies-in-malaysia-item-5"></a>**Add a cover letter** (Surat Iringan) and the employer's letter <sup>[210 (2023)](#o210)</sup>.
6. <a id="step-2-the-sponsor-applies-in-malaysia-item-6"></a>**Submit in person;** the sponsor signs the sponsor form before an Immigration officer <sup>[209](#o209), [210 (2023)](#o210)</sup>. VDRs are decided by the Visa, Pass and Permit Division, Immigration headquarters, No. 15, Tingkat 1-7, Blok 2G4, Persiaran Perdana, Presint 2, 62550 Putrajaya; 03-8000 8000 <sup>[209](#o209), [214](#o214)</sup>. Incomplete applications or ones without documentary proof are not accepted <sup>[209](#o209)</sup>.
7. <a id="step-2-the-sponsor-applies-in-malaysia-item-7"></a>**Wait about 7 working days;** the result is a visa approval letter (eVAL) <sup>[209](#o209), [210 (2023)](#o210), [201](#o201), [3](#o3)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does a Bangladeshi relative's sponsor pay a cash deposit (the form asks about one, wang cagaran, and IMI's bond rate for Bangladeshis is RM750) <sup>[210 (2023)](#o210), [215](#o215)</sup>?
> - What must the cover letter contain?
> - Does a state Immigration office also take the application?
> - What are the counter hours?
> - Is there any IMI fee for the approval?
> - How does the approval reach the relative?
> - Can a refusal be appealed?

<a id="step-3-apply-for-the-evisa"></a>

### 7.3 Step 3: Apply for the eVISA

Bangladeshis still need an eVISA with the VDR approval <sup>[216](#o216)</sup>. You'll need <sup>[3](#o3), [185](#o185)</sup>:

- a scan of your passport's data page;
- the approval letter;
- an email address;
- a card or e-wallet.

1. Apply on [malaysiavisa.imi.gov.my](https://malaysiavisa.imi.gov.my/) as in [Tourist visa, Step 2: Apply on MyVISA](#step-2-apply-on-myvisa), choosing Visa With Reference, with details exactly as on the approval letter; any difference must be corrected by the issuing office before you pay <sup>[3](#o3), [4](#o4), [201](#o201)</sup>.
2. Pay the same fees as a tourist eVISA; nothing is refunded <sup>[185](#o185), [3](#o3)</sup>. If charged twice, or the status stays "New Application", don't pay again; use MyVISA's 24-hour Live Chat <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>.
3. Download, print and check the eVISA as in [Tourist visa, Step 2: Apply on MyVISA](#step-2-apply-on-myvisa) and [Tourist visa, Step 3: Check your visa is real](#step-3-check-your-visa-is-real) <sup>[3](#o3), [205](#o205)</sup>.

<a id="step-4-travel"></a>

### 7.4 Step 4: Travel

Register the MDAC (Malaysia Digital Arrival Card) within 3 days before arrival and carry the papers the border officer checks (see [Tourist visa, Step 5: At the border](#step-5-at-the-border)) <sup>[4](#o4), [3](#o3)</sup>.

<a id="can-you-work-2"></a>

### 7.5 Can you work?

<a id="if-the-visa-is-refused-2"></a>

### 7.6 If the visa is refused

<a id="scams-3"></a>

### 7.7 Scams

Working ends a visit pass (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)); Immigration arrests Bangladeshis working on visit passes, and the sponsor loses the bond <sup>[51](#o51), [39 (2006)](#o39), [N179](#n179), [N180](#n180), [213](#o213)</sup>. False statements are an offence (see [Tourist visa (social visit), Tried by travellers](#tried-by-travellers)) <sup>[39 (2006)](#o39)</sup>. Immigration matters go only through Immigration counters or its official portal; no officer or third party may handle them outside those channels <sup>[N181](#n181)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What scams run on this route, such as fake sponsors or brokers charging for a "family VDR"?

If a sponsor or broker cheats you:

1. <a id="scams-3-item-1"></a>**Before paying,** check a phone number, bank account or company name on the Royal Malaysia Police's Semak Mule portal <sup>[217](#o217)</sup>.
2. <a id="scams-3-item-2"></a>**In Malaysia,** report at the nearest police station; in an emergency call 999, free <sup>[217](#o217), [197](#o197)</sup>. If you paid online, first call the National Scam Response Centre (NSRC), 997, 24 hours, within 24 hours so the money can be blocked, and tell your bank <sup>[217](#o217)</sup>.
3. <a id="scams-3-item-3"></a>**If an official asked for a bribe,** report to the MACC: 1-800-88-6000 (free), [portaladuan.sprm.gov.my](https://portaladuan.sprm.gov.my/), info@sprm.gov.my, SMS +6019-6000 696; No. 2, Lebuh Wawasan, Presint 7, 62250 Putrajaya <sup>[218](#o218)</sup>.
4. <a id="scams-3-item-4"></a>**For help as a Bangladeshi,** contact the Bangladesh High Commission in Kuala Lumpur (see [Contacts](#contacts)) <sup>[125](#o125), [107](#o107)</sup>.

<a id="business-visit"></a>

## 8 Business visit

A social visit pass covers meetings, conferences, inspections, audits, signing agreements, investment surveys and seminars, not professional or technical work or a job <sup>[208](#o208), [219](#o219), [39 (2006)](#o39)</sup>. For up to 30 days of urgent professional work the Malaysian company gets PLS@Xpats permission (see [PLS@XPATS](#plsxpats)); longer work needs a Professional Visit Pass or Employment Pass <sup>[220 (2022)](#o220), [221](#o221)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-get-the-papers"></a>

### 8.1 Step 1: Get the papers

You'll need: the papers in [Tourist visa, Step 1: Prepare](#step-1-prepare), plus your company's dispatch letter or an invitation from a Malaysian government body or government-linked company <sup>[4](#o4), [3](#o3)</sup>.

<a id="step-2-apply-for-the-business-evisa-on-myvisa"></a>

### 8.2 Step 2: Apply for the business eVISA on MyVISA

Apply, pay and check the eVISA as in [Tourist visa, Step 2: Apply on MyVISA](#step-2-apply-on-myvisa) and [Tourist visa, Step 3: Check your visa is real](#step-3-check-your-visa-is-real), choosing the business purpose and uploading the letter and ticket; the fees, the 2 to 7 working days and the refusal rules are the same <sup>[3](#o3), [205](#o205), [185](#o185)</sup>.

A business multiple-entry visa (6 months, up to 30 days per entry) is limited to 31 eligible nationalities <sup>[201](#o201), [3](#o3)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is Bangladesh among the 31 nationalities eligible for the business multiple-entry visa (it is on IMI's separate list of 31 nationalities that need an eVISA) <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>?

<a id="can-you-work-3"></a>

### 8.3 Can you work?

<a id="if-the-visa-is-refused-3"></a>

### 8.4 If the visa is refused

<a id="scams-4"></a>

### 8.5 Scams

Fake invitations: a syndicate sold Bangladeshis fake invitation letters (such as to sports competitions), with return tickets and hotel bookings to fool officers, for RM2,000 to RM5,000 <sup>[N182 (2024)](#n182)</sup>. Don't pay anyone to "get" the visa: it is applied for and paid only online, for the [Step 2: Apply for the business eVISA on MyVISA](#step-2-apply-for-the-business-evisa-on-myvisa) fees, and no officer or third party may handle it outside official channels <sup>[3](#o3), [N181](#n181)</sup>. False statements are an offence (see [Tourist visa (social visit), Tried by travellers](#tried-by-travellers)) <sup>[39 (2006)](#o39)</sup>.

If cheated:

- **In Malaysia:** use the route under [Family visit sponsored by a Malaysian citizen (VDR), Scams](#scams-3) <sup>[217](#o217), [218](#o218)</sup>.
- **In Bangladesh,** if an agency or broker took money for a "business visa" job: complain to BMET (see [Work visa: company worker (PLKS), If an agency or broker takes your money](#if-an-agency-or-broker-takes-your-money)) <sup>[1](#o1), [100](#o100)</sup>.

<a id="medical-visit"></a>

## 9 Medical visit

The medical eVISA is only for treatment at a member hospital of MHTC (Malaysia Healthcare Travel Council, the Health Ministry's agency for foreign patients) <sup>[3](#o3), [222](#o222)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-get-a-hospital-appointment"></a>

### 9.1 Step 1: Get a hospital appointment

You'll need <sup>[223](#o223)</sup>:

- medical records and test results;
- recent scans (X-ray, CT, MRI), digitally or on CD;
- a written medical history;
- your medicines, with generic names and doses.

1. Choose a hospital under "Find A Hospital" on [malaysiahealthcare.org](https://malaysiahealthcare.org/find-hospital-filter); MHTC's concierge, WhatsApp +60 18 870 0100, helps choose one and can arrange the appointment, hotel and transport <sup>[224](#o224), [3](#o3), [225](#o225)</sup>.
2. Send your records and ask for an appointment letter in English with treatment and personal details, a written cost estimate for treatment, stay and follow-up, and an MHTC support letter for the visa <sup>[224](#o224), [223](#o223)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does a hospital or MHTC charge for reviewing records or issuing the appointment letter?
   > - How long does it take to get the appointment letter?
   > - How can you check an appointment letter is genuine?

<a id="step-2-apply-for-the-medical-evisa"></a>

### 9.2 Step 2: Apply for the medical eVISA

You'll need <sup>[224](#o224)</sup>:

- a passport valid at least 6 months;
- the appointment letter;
- the MHTC support letter;
- a return ticket;
- proof of accommodation;
- a photo.

Fees and refusal rules are as in [Tourist visa, Step 2: Apply on MyVISA](#step-2-apply-on-myvisa); a decision takes 3 to 5 working days <sup>[3](#o3), [185](#o185), [224](#o224)</sup>.

1. Apply on [malaysiavisa.imi.gov.my](https://malaysiavisa.imi.gov.my/) at least 14 working days before the appointment (see [Tourist visa, Step 2: Apply on MyVISA](#step-2-apply-on-myvisa)) <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>. MHTC names Irving Aviation Ltd as its local partner in Bangladesh (contact Abid Rahman Rafsan, +8801762001467) <sup>[222](#o222)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Must Bangladeshis go through MHTC's partners, as the High Commission in Dhaka says <sup>[61](#o61)</sup>, or can they apply themselves?
   > - What does MHTC's partner in Bangladesh charge?

2. The approval comes by email; print it and keep a copy on your phone <sup>[224](#o224)</sup>.

A medical multiple-entry visa, where given, lasts 6 months <sup>[201](#o201)</sup>.

<a id="step-3-companions-and-arrival"></a>

### 9.3 Step 3: Companions and arrival

Up to 2 companions may come (spouse, parent, child, grandparent, sibling, nurse or domestic helper) <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>. At the airport show <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>:

- passport;
- eVISA;
- eVAL printout;
- appointment letter;
- companions' details.

The special airport lane is only for MHTC member-hospital patients with an MHTC authorisation letter or a Meet and Greet package <sup>[224](#o224)</sup>; Meet and Greet costs RM189 per companion, and the hospital must request it at least 3 working days ahead <sup>[224](#o224)</sup>.

<a id="how-long-you-can-stay-1"></a>

### 9.4 How long you can stay

For a government or specialist hospital, IMI gives a Long-Term Social Visit Pass, with one companion <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>. The patient, hospital or companion applies with <sup>[226 (2023)](#o226), [227](#o227)</sup>:

- Forms Imm.38 (IMI's visa application form) and Imm.12;
- every printed passport page;
- the hospital's support letter;
- 3 months' bank statements;
- proof the companion is close family, certified by the Malaysian High Commission;
- the MHTC letter;
- proof of payment.

It takes 25 working days <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which IMI office takes a Long-Term Social Visit Pass application for treatment?
> - What is the fee?
> - What document do you get?
> - Can a refusal be appealed?

<a id="can-you-work-4"></a>

### 9.5 Can you work?

<a id="if-the-visa-is-refused-4"></a>

### 9.6 If the visa is refused

<a id="scams-5"></a>

### 9.7 Scams

No unauthorised work; false statements and using another's pass are offences (see [Tourist visa (social visit), Tried by travellers](#tried-by-travellers)) <sup>[201](#o201), [39 (2006)](#o39)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What scams target Bangladeshis on the Malaysia medical-visa route?

- **About the hospital** (care, staff or bill): complain to the Health Ministry's Private Medical Practice Control Section (CKAPS) at [myckaps.spab.gov.my](https://myckaps.spab.gov.my/eApps/system/index.do), 03-8883 1362, ckaps.aduan@moh.gov.my, or Deputy Director CKAPS, Level 3, Block E1, Complex E, 62590 Putrajaya <sup>[228](#o228), [229](#o229)</sup>. Family may complain with the patient's written permission. The hospital must reply within 14 days of CKAPS's letter, simple cases take 30 days, and a refund or bill reduction is up to the hospital, a lawyer or a civil court <sup>[228](#o228), [229](#o229)</sup>.
- **About an agent who took money:** use the police, NSRC and MACC route ([Family visit sponsored by a Malaysian citizen (VDR), Scams](#scams-3)) <sup>[217](#o217), [218](#o218)</sup>.

<a id="professional-visit-pass"></a>

## 10 Professional Visit Pass

The Professional Visit Pass (PVP) lets a professional sent by an overseas company work on a project or train with a Malaysian company for up to 12 months; it isn't for general workers or operators. You work only for the named company, with no Dependant Pass for family <sup>[221](#o221), [219](#o219)</sup>. You can't apply yourself: a registered Malaysian company, organisation or institution applies while you are in Bangladesh <sup>[219](#o219)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-the-malaysian-company-prepares"></a>

### 10.1 Step 1: The Malaysian company prepares

1. The company registers with ESD <sup>[221](#o221)</sup>.
2. On Xpats Gateway (the employer-government platform for Employment Pass and PVP applications) it gets JTKSM (Labour Department of Peninsular Malaysia) approval to employ a foreigner under section 60K of the Employment Act, and acknowledgement that it advertised on MYFutureJobs, the job portal of PERKESO (the Social Security Organisation) <sup>[230](#o230), [231](#o231)</sup>. A training organiser applies before you travel <sup>[51](#o51)</sup>.
3. **Check the company** on SSM e-Info as in [Work visa: company worker (PLKS), Step 1, item 7](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-7) <sup>[50](#o50), [83](#o83)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How can you check a company's ESD registration?
   > - How can you check a PVP offer is genuine?

<a id="step-2-send-your-documents-the-company-applies"></a>

### 10.2 Step 2: Send your documents; the company applies

You'll need <sup>[219](#o219), [232](#o232)</sup>:

- passport copy;
- appointment or assignment letter;
- invitation or support letter;
- work or training schedule;
- contract or project papers;
- academic or professional certificates, prepared as in [Employment Pass, Step 1: The employer applies](#step-1-the-employer-applies);
- proof of experience.

The company applies through ESD and pays RM1,200 plus tax, then RM90 per quarter of the pass <sup>[221](#o221), [233](#o233)</sup>. The bond for Bangladeshis is RM750, by bank guarantee to the Director General of Immigration <sup>[215](#o215), [234 (2024)](#o234)</sup>.

The period approved depends on the task; 12 months is not automatic <sup>[219](#o219)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long does a standard PVP decision take?

Only the company can appeal a refusal, within 14 days in ESD Online; appeals are decided within 30 working days <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>.

<a id="step-3-bangladesh-clearance"></a>

### 10.3 Step 3: Bangladesh clearance

> **Need to know**
> - Does a PVP holder need BMET emigration clearance, as a worker with a self-obtained visa does (see [Employment Pass, Step 2: Emigration clearance in Bangladesh](#step-2-emigration-clearance-in-bangladesh)) <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>?

<a id="step-4-enter-and-get-the-epass"></a>

### 10.4 Step 4: Enter and get the ePASS

1. After approval you get a VDR to enter <sup>[236](#o236), [235](#o235)</sup>.
2. The company pays for the endorsement in ESD Online; then either <sup>[235](#o235), [237](#o237)</sup>:
   - you go, no appointment needed, to the Expatriate Satellite Centre at KLIA Terminal 1 or 2; or
   - the company gets it endorsed at the MYXpats Centre (TalentCorp and Immigration): Level 4, Surian Tower, No 1, Jalan PJU 7/3, Mutiara Damansara, 47810 Petaling Jaya, Selangor; 9 am to 5 pm, Monday to Friday.
3. Always carry the ePASS with your passport <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>. Check it by its QR code or at [imigresen-online.imi.gov.my/eservices/epass?semakan](https://imigresen-online.imi.gov.my/eservices/epass?semakan) <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>. Mistakes are corrected by the company through the MYXpats helpdesk (+603-7839 7171, helpdesk@myxpats.com.my) in 3 to 5 working days <sup>[235](#o235), [237](#o237)</sup>.

**Scams:** a work pass sold through a middleman for RM5,000 had a tampered QR code and no record in Immigration's system <sup>[N181](#n181)</sup>; check every pass as in [Step 4, item 3](#step-4-enter-and-get-the-epass). False information can mean rejection, cancellation, withdrawal of the pass, immigration action and blacklisting <sup>[219](#o219)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Has a "PVP" ever been sold to general workers as a work visa?

Jockeys, volunteers and exchange or training students (up to 6 months) get PVPs from IMI outside ESD; artistes and film crews apply through PUSPAL, the Communications Ministry's filming centre <sup>[238](#o238)</sup>.

<a id="can-you-work-5"></a>

### 10.5 Can you work?

<a id="if-the-visa-is-refused-5"></a>

### 10.6 If the visa is refused

<a id="scams-6"></a>

### 10.7 Scams

<a id="transit"></a>

## 11 Transit

- **Same flight, staying in the airport:** no visa <sup>[239](#o239)</sup>.
- **Changing planes** on one booking (PNR) inside the transit hall: no visa. You need one to pass immigration (separate bookings, collecting bags, changing between KLIA and KLIA2 (Kuala Lumpur International Airport 2)) or to leave the airport <sup>[240](#o240), [57](#o57)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Does changing planes without a visa inside the transit hall apply to Bangladeshis as it does to Indians?

- **Leaving the airport on a layover:** apply on MyVISA for a transit or visit eVISA as in [Tourist visa, Step 2: Apply on MyVISA](#step-2-apply-on-myvisa) (same fees, time and refusal rules), with the airport as address and a cover letter explaining the layover <sup>[3](#o3), [185](#o185)</sup>. The 120-hour visa-free transit is suspended ([Visas not open to Bangladeshis](#visas-not-open-to-bangladeshis)) <sup>[58](#o58)</sup>.

**Ship crew:** a seaman signing on at a Malaysian port needs <sup>[241](#o241), [6](#o6)</sup>:

- a passport valid 6 months from entry;
- a Seaman Book;
- a Letter of Guarantee from IMI at the port;
- a transit visa.

> **Need to know**
> - Who requests the Letter of Guarantee?
> - Where is it requested?
> - What does it cost?
> - How long does it take?

<a id="employment-pass"></a>

## 12 Employment Pass

For professionals hired by a Malaysian company at a basic salary of at least RM5,000 a month who meet ESD's qualification rule ([Who can apply](#who-can-apply)); valid for one employer, and only the employer applies <sup>[16](#o16), [232](#o232), [235](#o235), [242](#o242)</sup>. A pass issued through ESD is valid only in Peninsular Malaysia, and changing company means a new application <sup>[242](#o242)</sup>. Some passes are approved by other bodies <sup>[243](#o243), [244](#o244), [245](#o245)</sup>:

- manufacturing: MIDA (Malaysian Investment Development Authority);
- ICT companies with MSC Malaysia status: the Multimedia Development Corporation;
- banking, finance and insurance: Bank Negara Malaysia (the central bank);
- the capital market: the Securities Commission;
- government contract posts: the Public Services Department;
- the East Coast Economic Region: ECERDC (East Coast Economic Region Development Council);
- Iskandar Malaysia: IRDA (Iskandar Regional Development Authority).

> **Need to know**
> - What is MSC Malaysia status?
> - How does a company get it?

<a id="who-can-apply"></a>

### 12.1 Who can apply

The category depends on your basic salary <sup>[16](#o16), [246](#o246)</sup>:

- Category I: RM20,000 a month or more, up to 10 years;
- Category II: RM10,000 to RM19,999, up to 10 years;
- Category III: RM5,000 to RM9,999 (RM7,000 to RM9,999 in manufacturing), up to 5 years, counting time with the same employer, position and category.

Only basic salary taxable in Malaysia counts, not allowances, bonuses or pay outside Malaysia <sup>[16](#o16), [246](#o246)</sup>.

You need one of <sup>[232](#o232)</sup>:

- a degree and 3 years' relevant experience;
- a diploma and 5;
- a technical certificate and 7.

All categories may bring family (see [Family of an Employment Pass holder](#family-of-an-employment-pass-holder)) <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-the-employer-applies"></a>

### 12.2 Step 1: The employer applies

You'll need <sup>[232](#o232)</sup>:

- a passport valid more than 12 months with 6 empty pages, the whole booklet and both covers scanned in colour;
- an unedited photo, 3.5 x 5.0 cm, light blue background, 99 x 142 pixels;
- your highest educational (and any professional) certificate and a detailed resume with your total work experience, in colour;
- the contract, stamped by the Inland Revenue Board and signed and dated by both sides.

From Bangladesh, English certificates must be certified true copies from an embassy or high commission (in Bangladesh or Malaysia) or the employer's HR manager; others must be translated into English and certified by the Malaysian High Commission in Dhaka <sup>[232](#o232)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What does the Malaysian High Commission in Dhaka charge to certify certificates?
> - How long does it take?

1. <a id="step-1-the-employer-applies-item-1"></a>**The employer registers with ESD** (5 working days), then gets JTKSM approval and the MYFutureJobs acknowledgement (see [Professional Visit Pass, Step 1: The Malaysian company prepares](#step-1-the-malaysian-company-prepares)), in 10 working days; hiring without approval risks a fine up to RM100,000, 5 years' jail or both <sup>[16](#o16), [230](#o230), [231](#o231)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-1-the-employer-applies-item-2"></a>**Send the employer** the documents above <sup>[232](#o232)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-1-the-employer-applies-item-3"></a>**The employer applies on ESD Online and pays RM2,160;** the decision takes 2 to 5 working days <sup>[233](#o233), [16](#o16), [235](#o235)</sup>. Only the employer can appeal a refusal, within 14 days, or else claim 75% of the processing fee back within 14 days <sup>[235](#o235), [247 (2024)](#o247)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-1-the-employer-applies-item-4"></a>**The employer applies for your VDR** with the pass; after approval it pays the endorsement: RM200 a year, RM125 processing and RM20 a year visa fee <sup>[235](#o235), [233](#o233), [248](#o248)</sup>.

<a id="step-2-emigration-clearance-in-bangladesh"></a>

### 12.3 Step 2: Emigration clearance in Bangladesh

You'll need <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>:

- your application;
- a visa copy;
- the contract if any;
- an attested copy of your OEP registration card (see [Work visa: company worker (PLKS), Step 1, item 1](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-1));
- a declaration on Tk 300 non-judicial stamp paper;
- a pay-order for the fees.

Apply yourself at BMET's emigration branch in Dhaka (BMET head office: 89/2 Kakrail, Dhaka-1000) or the District Employment and Manpower Office in Chattogram; it takes 3 working days <sup>[1](#o1), [96 (2021)](#o96)</sup>. Fees and the smart card checked at the airport are as in [Work visa: company worker (PLKS), Step 6: Orientation, BMET clearance and the flight](#step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-and-the-flight) <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>. Problems: Director (Emigration), +8802222222415, diremi@bmet.gov.bd <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What are the emigration branch's opening hours?
> - Where do you buy the Tk 300 stamp paper?
> - Which bank is the pay-order drawn on?
> - What can you do if clearance is refused?

<a id="step-3-fly-and-get-the-epass"></a>

### 12.4 Step 3: Fly and get the ePASS

1. Register the MDAC within 3 days before arrival <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>.
2. On arrival go, no appointment needed, to the Expatriate Satellite Centre at KLIA Terminal 1 or 2 for your ePASS and i-KAD (the Employment Pass holder's card) <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>.

Pass problems go to the MYXpats helpdesk (see [Professional Visit Pass, Step 4: Enter and get the ePASS](#step-4-enter-and-get-the-epass)) <sup>[237](#o237)</sup>.

<a id="step-4-check-your-visa-is-real-1"></a>

### 12.5 Step 4: Check your visa is real

- Check the ePASS by QR code or at [imigresen-online.imi.gov.my/eservices/epass?semakan](https://imigresen-online.imi.gov.my/eservices/epass?semakan), since fakes are sold (see [Professional Visit Pass, Scams](#scams-6)) <sup>[235](#o235), [N181](#n181)</sup>.
- Check the employer on SSM e-Info before accepting <sup>[50](#o50)</sup>.

If you get a new passport, the company must transfer the pass endorsement to it, or Immigration may stop you leaving <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>. If cheated, use the route under [Family visit sponsored by a Malaysian citizen (VDR), Scams](#scams-3) <sup>[217](#o217), [218](#o218)</sup>. False statements and using another's pass are offences (see [Tourist visa (social visit), Tried by travellers](#tried-by-travellers)) <sup>[39 (2006)](#o39)</sup>.

<a id="can-you-work-6"></a>

### 12.6 Can you work?

<a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1"></a>

### 12.7 If something goes wrong

Unpaid wages and other Employment Act breaches go through the Working for Workers app, the Bangladesh High Commission's labour wing and the Labour Court (see [If you aren't paid](#if-you-arent-paid)) <sup>[105](#o105), [130](#o130), [97 (2017)](#o97)</sup>. JTKSM advises free on 03-8000 8000 or jtksm@mohr.gov.my <sup>[97 (2017)](#o97)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What can an Employment Pass holder do if the employer cancels or doesn't renew the pass?

<a id="if-the-visa-is-refused-6"></a>

### 12.8 If the visa is refused

<a id="scams-7"></a>

### 12.9 Scams

<a id="family-passes"></a>

## 13 Family passes

The pass depends on who brings the family member: an Employment Pass holder, a Malaysian citizen, a permanent resident or a student.

For a student's family, see [Student visa (Student Pass), Bringing your family](#bringing-your-family-1).

<a id="family-of-an-employment-pass-holder"></a>

### 13.1 Family of an Employment Pass holder

**Who gets which pass** <sup>[16](#o16), [194](#o194)</sup>:

- **Dependant Pass:** a spouse and children under 18.
- **Long-Term Social Visit Pass** (LTSVP, a pass for a stay of 6 months or more): parents, parents-in-law, children aged 18 to 25 and a common-law spouse.

A Dependant Pass lasts as long as the Employment Pass, or until the dependant's passport expires if sooner <sup>[249](#o249)</sup>. Holders of a Category III Employment Pass issued before 1 June 2026 (when Category III covered a basic salary of RM3,000 to RM4,999) still can't bring dependants <sup>[246](#o246), [16](#o16)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long is an LTSVP for these relatives approved for at a time?

1. <a id="family-of-an-employment-pass-holder-item-1"></a>**Get the family documents translated and attested.**

   You'll need <sup>[232](#o232)</sup>:

   - for a spouse, the marriage certificate (in Bangladesh, the Muslim marriage registration certificate);
   - each child's birth certificate;
   - each person's passport;
   - for a common-law spouse, an affidavit or acknowledgement letter from the Employment Pass holder's embassy.

   1. Translate each certificate into English if needed <sup>[232](#o232)</sup>. Take the original and translation, both with the translation agency's and translator's seal and signature, to an approved notary public and then the Ministry of Law <sup>[250 (2022)](#o250)</sup>.
   2. Each certificate must then be certified as a true copy, dated in the current year, by the Malaysian High Commission in Dhaka or the Bangladesh High Commission in Kuala Lumpur <sup>[232](#o232)</sup>.
   3. The Bangladesh High Commission attests a marriage certificate only after the Ministry of Law (sub-step 1) and then the Ministry of Foreign Affairs have attested it in Dhaka <sup>[251 (2024)](#o251), [250 (2022)](#o250)</sup>. For the Ministry of Foreign Affairs, hand in two sets at the Ministry of Foreign Affairs' Consular wing in Dhaka (rooms 201/205; 02-47123389; asconsular2@mofa.gov.bd) between 8 am and noon with a copy of your NID or passport, and collect them free the same day, 2 to 3 pm <sup>[250 (2022)](#o250)</sup>. A birth certificate must be issued through BDRIS (Bangladesh's online birth and death registration system), verifiable online, and attested by a notary and the Ministry of Law <sup>[250 (2022)](#o250)</sup>; for a birth certificate the High Commission also accepts proof of online verification instead of the Dhaka attestation <sup>[251 (2024)](#o251)</sup>.
   4. At the Bangladesh High Commission (No 8, Lorong Yap Kwan Seng, 50450 Kuala Lumpur), bring the attested originals, copies of every paper and of your passport and visa, and one extra set of copies <sup>[251 (2024)](#o251), [125](#o125)</sup>. Attesting a certificate or affidavit costs RM42 (RM63 urgent) plus RM8.40 per extra page, paid by card at the counter (Maybank Merchant Card Service) or into the High Commission's Maybank account 564427102268 with the original deposit slip <sup>[252](#o252)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What are the Bangladesh High Commission's counter hours for attestation?
   > - How long does its attestation take?
   > - What is the Malaysian High Commission in Dhaka's procedure for certifying family documents?
   > - What is its fee?

2. <a id="family-of-an-employment-pass-holder-item-2"></a>**The employer applies.** Only the Employment Pass holder's company can apply, on ESD Online, with the Employment Pass or after it is approved <sup>[249](#o249), [235](#o235)</sup>.

   You'll need, to give the employer <sup>[232](#o232)</sup>:

   - the attested documents;
   - a colour scan of each family member's passport;
   - for an LTSVP, a personal bond form stamped by LHDN.

   The employer pays <sup>[233](#o233), [248](#o248)</sup>:

   - RM540 per person on applying;
   - then RM90 a year for the pass;
   - RM50 processing;
   - the visa fee (RM20 a year for a Bangladeshi).

   The LTSVP bond is a promise that the relative will obey immigration law, backed by a Malaysian company representative's pledge of RM750 for a Bangladeshi <sup>[232](#o232), [215](#o215)</sup>. A decision takes about 14 working days; the family member gets an ePASS (electronic pass) <sup>[232](#o232), [235](#o235)</sup>.

   **Check it is real:** scan the ePASS QR code or check it at [imigresen-online.imi.gov.my/eservices/epass?semakan](https://imigresen-online.imi.gov.my/eservices/epass?semakan) <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>. A false statement to get a pass is an offence <sup>[39 (2006)](#o39)</sup>. **If refused:** ESD's online appeal covers only Employment Pass and Professional Visit Pass refusals <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>. Questions: MYXpats Centre, which processes Employment Pass applications <sup>[253](#o253)</sup>, +603-7839 7171, helpdesk@myxpats.com.my <sup>[254](#o254)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Is the RM750 a deposit?
   > - Is the RM750 returned?
   > - Can a refused Dependant Pass or LTSVP be appealed?

3. <a id="family-of-an-employment-pass-holder-item-3"></a>**Work and study.** To work, a Dependant Pass holder needs their own Employment Pass <sup>[249](#o249), [232](#o232)</sup>. From 1 January 2027 the spouse of a Category I Employment Pass holder (RM20,000 a month or more) may work on the Dependant Pass, under conditions still to be set <sup>[255](#o255), [16](#o16)</sup>. A spouse on a Dependant Pass applies for Permission to Study online through the Sub-Product menu in ESD Online; paper applications are no longer accepted at Immigration or MYXpats Centre counters <sup>[254](#o254)</sup>. LTSVP relatives may not work or study without switching to an Employment Pass or Student Pass <sup>[232](#o232)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What conditions apply to Category I spouses working on the Dependant Pass, once published?
   > - What is the Permission to Study fee?
   > - How long does Permission to Study take?
   > - What does a Permission to Study holder receive?

> **Need to know**
> - Is there any official warning about scams in family-pass applications?

<a id="spouse-of-a-malaysian-citizen"></a>

### 13.2 Spouse of a Malaysian citizen

<a id="before-you-marry"></a>

#### 13.2.1 Before you marry

A PLKS worker may not marry a Malaysian or another foreign worker in Malaysia; one who does loses the work permit and is sent home <sup>[9](#o9), [N183 (2023)](#n183)</sup>. For other foreigners, the Malaysian partner first has Immigration confirm the foreigner's pass on IMI's pass-confirmation form for marriage, [Permohonan Pengesahan Pas bagi Tujuan Perkahwinan Warganegara Asing](https://www.imi.gov.my/wp-content/uploads/2022/02/Borang-Pengesahan-Pas-Bagi-Tujuan-Perkahwinan-Dengan-Warganegara-Asing.pdf) <sup>[256 (2022)](#o256)</sup>. Hand in two copies at any state Immigration headquarters with <sup>[256 (2022)](#o256)</sup>:

- a copy of the partner's identity card;
- two passport photos of each of you;
- a copy of the foreigner's passport with the pass pages.

An officer certifies the pass and its expiry on the form <sup>[256 (2022)](#o256)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does the pass-confirmation form have a fee?
> - How long does it take?

The Bangladesh High Commission in Kuala Lumpur asks a Bangladeshi for <sup>[251 (2024)](#o251)</sup>:

- an unmarried certificate from the Deputy Commissioner's office, attested by the Ministry of Foreign Affairs in Dhaka;
- the parents' affidavit or consent, attested by the Ministry of Law and the Ministry of Foreign Affairs;
- the consular fee receipt.

The marriage must be registered under Malaysian law before the spouse can get permission to work or apply for permanent residence <sup>[257](#o257), [157](#o157)</sup>.

1. <a id="before-you-marry-item-1"></a>**Apply for the spouse's LTSVP.**

   You'll need, as originals with copies <sup>[194](#o194), [258](#o258)</sup>:

   - IMI's forms, listed on IMI's [new spouse application page](https://www.imi.gov.my/index.php/permohonan-baru-isteri-atau-suami-kepada-warganegara/):
     - [Form Imm.12](http://www.imi.gov.my/wp-content/uploads/2022/02/IM.12.pdf);
     - [Form Imm.38](http://www.imi.gov.my/wp-content/uploads/2022/03/IM38.pdf) (2 copies);
     - [Form Imm.55](http://www.imi.gov.my/wp-content/uploads/2022/03/Imm55.pdf) (2 copies);
     - [sponsor's statement](http://www.imi.gov.my/wp-content/uploads/2022/03/Borang_Kenyatan_Penaja.pdf);
     - [security bond](http://www.imi.gov.my/wp-content/uploads/2022/03/Security-Bond.pdf);
     - [sworn declaration](http://www.imi.gov.my/wp-content/uploads/2022/03/Surat_Akuan_Sumpah.pdf);
   - the Malaysian spouse's identity card (MyKad), birth certificate and passport (all pages);
   - the marriage registration certificate;
   - any divorce or death certificate from an earlier marriage;
   - a postcard-size wedding photo;
   - children's birth certificates;
   - the Malaysian spouse's employer letter (or business licence if self-employed);
   - proof of income (3 months' payslips, bank book or tax Form J);
   - a utility bill.

   1. Stamp the security bond with a RM10 revenue stamp <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>; have the sworn declaration, with your photo, stamped by a Commissioner for Oaths <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>.
   2. Go with the Malaysian spouse to the Immigration office nearest your home <sup>[259](#o259), [258](#o258)</sup>. Immigration checks every original and accepts only complete applications <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>.
   3. A decision takes 25 working days under IMI's client charter; the pass runs 6 months to 5 years at a time <sup>[88](#o88), [194](#o194)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is the fee for the spouse LTSVP?
   > - Can a refusal be appealed?

   **Checks and scams.** Immigration refuses a marriage made only for immigration status and looks closely at large age gaps <sup>[N184](#n184)</sup>; it may visit your address, interview people and check documents before granting or extending the pass <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>. Anyone selling a spouse pass or marriage registration is a syndicate <sup>[N184](#n184)</sup>; report them (see [If Immigration mishandles any family-pass application](#if-immigration-mishandles-any-family-pass-application)) <sup>[260](#o260), [261](#o261)</sup>. A false statement in the application can bring a fine of up to RM10,000, up to 5 years' jail, or both <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>.

2. <a id="before-you-marry-item-2"></a>**Get permission to work.** Once endorsed in the passport, it lets the spouse work, run a business or practise a profession on the LTSVP <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>.

   You'll need:

   - the Malaysian spouse in person with their MyKad;
   - your passport and pass;
   - a sworn statement;
   - the marriage certificate;
   - the job offer;
   - an employment contract with a RM10 revenue stamp stating the job, period and monthly pay <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>;
   - IMI's [Applicant, Citizen Spouse and Employer Information Form](http://www.imi.gov.my/wp-content/uploads/2022/03/Borang_Maklumat.pdf);
   - the employer's latest company registration form from MyData SSM (the online records of the Companies Commission of Malaysia) <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>.

   Apply at the Visa, Pass and Permit Division of Immigration in the state where you'll work, with the LTSVP application or any time after a job offer <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Can the work permission also be applied for at Immigration headquarters in Putrajaya or the nearest state office?
   > - What is its fee?
   > - How long does it take?

<a id="permanent-residence"></a>

#### 13.2.2 Permanent residence

A wife can apply for an Entry Permit after 3 years of marriage and over 12 months on the spouse LTSVP; a husband after 5 years of marriage and 5 continuous years on it <sup>[157](#o157)</sup>. See [Permanent residence (Entry Permit)](#permanent-residence-entry-permit).

**Residence Pass** (Pas Residen: RM500 for 5 years; allows work, study and business) <sup>[262](#o262)</sup>. Who can get it <sup>[262](#o262)</sup>:

- relatives of citizens (biological, step or adopted children; a widowed or divorced spouse with a citizen child; parents and parents-in-law), after 3 years on a valid long-term pass;
- children and spouses of permanent residents, after 5.

You'll need:

- Form IMM 16A <sup>[262](#o262)</sup>;
- 2 passport photos and a copy of the sponsor's identity card <sup>[262](#o262)</sup>;
- old and new passports and original supporting documents, with copies <sup>[262](#o262)</sup>;
- copies of Bangladeshi documents attested by the Bangladesh High Commission <sup>[262](#o262)</sup>.

1. A Malaysian citizen aged 21 or over sponsors you and comes with you to Immigration headquarters in Putrajaya or a nearby Immigration office <sup>[262](#o262)</sup>.
2. Pay RM500 plus a multiple-entry visa fee set by nationality <sup>[262](#o262)</sup>.
3. Track it on myOnlinePasResiden at [imigresen-online.imi.gov.my/eservices/main](https://imigresen-online.imi.gov.my/eservices/main) <sup>[188](#o188)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long does a Residence Pass decision take?
> - Can a refusal be appealed?

<a id="other-relatives-of-malaysians-and-of-permanent-residents"></a>

### 13.3 Other relatives of Malaysians and of permanent residents

IMI gives an LTSVP in other family categories <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>:

- A foreigner aged 60 or over with no family at home, sponsored by a child, child-in-law or sibling in Malaysia earning RM2,000 or more <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>, with a personal bond stamped RM10 at LHDN <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>.
- An abused, divorced or widowed wife of a citizen, with a local sponsor earning RM2,000 or more <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>; a widow's sponsor is a close relative of her late husband who comes with her <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>; an abused wife adds a police or hospital report, a security bond stamped RM10, and the sponsor's declaration and identity card <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>.
- The wife of a permanent resident, married over 6 months <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>.
- A citizen's child or stepchild aged 7 or under, on the citizen parent's application <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>.
- Close family of a foreign student, with the student's letter from the institution and an embassy letter proving the relationship <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>; see [Student visa (Student Pass), Bringing your family](#bringing-your-family-1).
- A patient at a government or specialist hospital, with one companion (see [Medical visit, How long you can stay](#how-long-you-can-stay-1)) <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>.

You'll need <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>:

- all originals;
- Forms Imm.12 (or Imm.55) and Imm.38;
- the marriage, divorce or birth certificates that apply.

Apply at the Immigration office nearest your home; only complete applications are accepted, and the charter gives 25 working days <sup>[259](#o259), [194](#o194), [88](#o88)</sup>. A dependant's or guardian's LTSVP costs RM90 <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. A false statement is an offence <sup>[39 (2006)](#o39)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - May these pass holders work?
> - What is the fee for the other categories?
> - Can a refusal be appealed?

<a id="if-immigration-mishandles-any-family-pass-application"></a>

#### 13.3.1 If Immigration mishandles any family-pass application

- **Complain online** on IMI's SISPAA at [imi.spab.gov.my/eApps/system/index.do](https://imi.spab.gov.my/eApps/system/index.do) <sup>[260](#o260)</sup>:
  1. Choose "Aduan/Maklum Balas Baharu" (new complaint).
  2. Fill in every field. For example: "I applied for a [pass] at [office] on [date], reference [number]. After [N] working days I have no decision, beyond IMI's client charter time (14 working days for a Dependant Pass, 25 for an LTSVP). Please tell me the status." <sup>[232](#o232), [88](#o88)</sup>
  3. Track it under "Semak Aduan/Maklum Balas" <sup>[260](#o260)</sup>.
- **An officer asks for money or misbehaves:** report to IMI's Integrity Division: Tingkat 4 (Menara), Persiaran Perdana, Presint 2, 62550 Putrajaya; aduanintegriti@imi.gov.my; 03-8880 1246. Giving your name and contact protects you under the Whistleblower Protection Act 2010 <sup>[261](#o261)</sup>.
- **Immigration headquarters:** No. 15, Persiaran Perdana, Presint 2, 62550 Putrajaya; 03-8000 8000 <sup>[263 (2022)](#o263)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long do SISPAA complaints take to answer?
> - Does the Bangladesh High Commission help with family-pass cases?

<a id="mm2h-malaysia-my-second-home"></a>

## 14 MM2H (Malaysia My Second Home)

A long-stay pass for people with substantial savings, run by MOTAC (the Ministry of Tourism, Arts and Culture), for citizens of any country with diplomatic relations with Malaysia <sup>[264](#o264)</sup>, aged 25 or over (21 for the SEZ/SFZ, Special Economic Zone / Special Financial Zone, tier) <sup>[264](#o264), [265](#o265)</sup>.

**Tiers** <sup>[266](#o266), [267](#o267)</sup>:

- **Silver:** USD 150,000 fixed deposit and a home of at least RM600,000; 5 years.
- **Gold:** USD 500,000 and RM1,000,000; 15 years.
- **Platinum:** USD 1,000,000 and RM2,000,000; 20 years.
- **SEZ/SFZ:** USD 65,000 (age 21 to 49) or USD 32,000 (50 and over) and a home in Forest City, Johor; 10 years <sup>[266](#o266), [265](#o265)</sup>.

**Family** <sup>[264](#o264), [266](#o266)</sup>:

- a spouse;
- children under 21 (biological, step or adopted);
- unmarried, unemployed children aged 21 to 34;
- disabled children of any age;
- parents and parents-in-law.

<a id="how-long-you-can-stay-2"></a>

### 14.1 How long you can stay

- Under 50, spend at least 90 days a year in Malaysia <sup>[264](#o264)</sup>.
- Only Platinum holders may work or run a business <sup>[268](#o268), [267](#o267), [269](#o269), [265](#o265)</sup>.
- Renewal is RM500 a year plus a visa fee of RM0 to RM50 by nationality <sup>[264](#o264)</sup>.
- MM2H years don't count toward permanent residence <sup>[157](#o157)</sup>.

<a id="can-you-work-7"></a>

### 14.2 Can you work?

<a id="if-the-visa-is-refused-7"></a>

### 14.3 If the visa is refused

Any appeal is for the Ministry of Home Affairs <sup>[264](#o264)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How is an MM2H refusal appealed to the Ministry of Home Affairs?
> - By when must the appeal be made?

<a id="scams-8"></a>

### 14.4 Scams

- Complain on MOTAC's SISPAA at [motac.spab.gov.my/eApps/system/index.do](https://motac.spab.gov.my/eApps/system/index.do) <sup>[270](#o270)</sup>:
  1. Choose "Aduan/Maklum Balas Baharu".
  2. Give the agent's name and licence number, and what you paid and when. For example: "I paid RM[amount] on [date] to [agent], licence MM2H[number], for an MM2H application. The agent has not applied or answered since [date]. Receipts attached. Please investigate and help me recover the money."
  3. Attach receipts.
  4. Track it under "Semak Aduan/Maklum Balas" <sup>[270](#o270)</sup>.
- Or call the MM2H office: +603 8891 7568 or +603 8891 7434; MOTAC, No. 2, Tower 1, Jalan P5/6, Presint 5, 62200 Putrajaya <sup>[264](#o264)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Has MOTAC ever recovered money for an applicant cheated by an agent?

<a id="sarawaks-s-mm2h"></a>

### 14.5 Sarawak's S-MM2H

Sarawak runs its own scheme, on these terms since 1 January 2025 <sup>[N185](#n185)</sup>:

- age 30 or over;
- RM500,000 fixed deposit in a Sarawak bank;
- 30 days a year in Sarawak;
- a 5-year pass;
- applied for through the state's one-stop centre or licensed agents.

1. <a id="sarawaks-s-mm2h-item-1"></a>**Check the money can legally leave Bangladesh.** Bangladesh Bank controls money leaving Bangladesh under the Foreign Exchange Regulation Act 1947 <sup>[271](#o271)</sup>; in 2024 it was reported not to have approved sending money abroad for MM2H <sup>[N186 (2024)](#n186)</sup>; a Malaysian MM2H licence doesn't by itself mean an agent meets Bangladesh's rules <sup>[N187](#n187)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is Bangladesh Bank's written rule on sending money abroad for MM2H?
   > - How do you ask Bangladesh Bank for permission?

2. <a id="sarawaks-s-mm2h-item-2"></a>**Choose a licensed agent.** Every application goes through an MM2H operator licensed by MOTAC <sup>[264](#o264)</sup>. Check at [mm2h.gov.my/agencies](https://www.mm2h.gov.my/agencies) that the company name, licence number (MM2H plus digits) and licence dates match what the agent shows <sup>[272](#o272)</sup>. No agent can guarantee approval; some have taken tens of thousands of ringgit, then stopped answering, and applicants couldn't recover it <sup>[N188 (2024)](#n188)</sup>.

3. <a id="sarawaks-s-mm2h-item-3"></a>**The agent applies** through the One Stop Centre MM2H on MOTAC's online system; the Ministry of Home Affairs approves through Immigration <sup>[264](#o264), [273](#o273)</sup>. Fees:
   - processing: RM5,000 for the main applicant and RM2,500 per dependant <sup>[266](#o266)</sup>;
   - participation: RM1,000 (Silver, SEZ/SFZ), RM3,000 (Gold) or RM200,000 (Platinum) <sup>[266](#o266)</sup>;
   - MOTAC's system also lists RM500 plus SST (sales and service tax) per application <sup>[273](#o273)</sup>.

   You need a Certificate of Good Conduct (in Bangladesh, a police clearance certificate; see Work visa: company worker (PLKS), [Step 4: Passport, police clearance and certificates](#step-4-passport-police-clearance-and-certificates)) and Malaysian police screening <sup>[N189 (2024)](#n189)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Is the RM500 in MOTAC's system extra to the processing and participation fees?
   > - What is the full document list?
   > - What is the processing time?

4. <a id="sarawaks-s-mm2h-item-4"></a>**After approval.**
   1. Everyone has a medical check at a MOTAC panel clinic or hospital <sup>[264](#o264)</sup>.
   2. Open the fixed deposit in a licensed Malaysian bank; up to half can later be withdrawn for a home, education, medical care or tourism in Malaysia <sup>[264](#o264)</sup>.
   3. Buy a home within a year of endorsement and keep it 10 years unless selling to buy a dearer one <sup>[274](#o274), [264](#o264)</sup>.

   Immigration's client charter gives 1 working day to endorse the pass <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.

<a id="de-rantau-nomad-pass"></a>

## 15 DE Rantau Nomad Pass

For remote workers and freelancers paid from outside Malaysia, run by MDEC (the Malaysia Digital Economy Corporation), for all nationalities except Israel, main applicant over 18 <sup>[275](#o275)</sup>. Minimum income: USD 36,000 a year in tech, USD 60,000 otherwise <sup>[275](#o275), [276](#o276)</sup>. Up to 12 months at a time and 48 in all <sup>[275](#o275)</sup>; holders at the two-year limit will get two more years under conditions to be set <sup>[255](#o255)</sup>. No route to permanent residence <sup>[276](#o276)</sup>. New applications are closed until 30 November 2026 and reopen on 1 December 2026 <sup>[277](#o277)</sup>.

**Family** <sup>[275](#o275)</sup>:

- a spouse or common-law partner;
- children under 18 (including adopted and stepchildren);
- a special-needs child of any age;
- parents.

A spouse can't work <sup>[275](#o275)</sup>.

1. <a id="de-rantau-nomad-pass-item-1"></a>**Prepare the documents** in English or with a certified translation, as clear PDFs, with the same name and income figures throughout <sup>[275](#o275), [278](#o278)</sup>.

   You'll need, as a remote employee:

   - a passport with 6 empty pages, valid over 14 months <sup>[278](#o278), [275](#o275)</sup>;
   - a CV;
   - a signed contract showing remote work, title, duration and salary, with an employer not registered in Malaysia, starting at least 3 months before you apply <sup>[278](#o278), [275](#o275)</sup>;
   - 3 months' payslips and bank statements showing the same deposits <sup>[278](#o278)</sup>;
   - an income statement or tax return;
